To overcome fragmented causal chains caused by missing events, the system dynamically calibrates query conditions to broaden the search scope. Concurrently, it performs precision result filtering and strategic nodes selection for subsequent exploration, thereby mitigating dependency explosions. Extensive evaluations in the wild demonstrate that SherAgent improves the end-to-end investigation success rate by 31.1% and 63.7% compared to both legacy enterprise baselines and SOTA approaches, respectively. Furthermore, it operates with remarkable efficiency, incurring under $0.10 in API costs and requiring less than 4 minutes per investigation. Finally, our user study confirms that SherAgent provides accurate and clear insights, significantly reducing the analytical overhead for security experts.

</details>


### [108] [Malaika: Understanding Malware through Tri-Grounded Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09179)
*Xingzhi Qian,Xinran Zheng,Yiling He,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出恶意代码理解需要三种互补的grounding（领域、语义、知识），并设计了多智能体框架Malaika加以实现，实验表明基于grounding的推理能产生更精确、可审计的分析结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统在安全代码分析上表现良好，但恶意代码理解面临独特挑战：分析者需要在部分可观测性下，从稀疏分散且与良性功能交织的证据中重构高层恶意行为。核心难点不在于识别可疑代码，而在于判断证据是否足以支撑可审计的行为级结论。

Method: 将恶意代码理解形式化为一个基于grounding的推理问题，提出三种互补的grounding机制：领域grounding（约束行为假设的生成与评估）、语义grounding（定位并连接支持性程序证据）、知识grounding（通过外部可验证威胁知识实现行为归因）。基于此设计Malaika多智能体框架，通过分析师启发的推理、工具介导的证据定位和检索增强的行为归因来操作化这三种机制，并在Android恶意代码分析场景中实例化。

Result: Malaika在恶意代码理解任务上的分析质量优于先前基于LLM的恶意代码分析框架；与恶意代码分析系统和前沿智能体框架的对比表明，grounding-aware推理能产生更精确、可审计的结论；消融实验进一步支持了grounding假设。

Conclusion: 可靠性不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于推理过程本身。Grounding-aware推理为可靠的恶意代码理解以及更广泛的、基于证据的软件分析提供了有原则的基础。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based systems have shown promising capabilities for security-focused code analysis. Malware understanding, however, poses a distinct challenge: analysts must reconstruct high-level malicious behaviors under partial observability from sparse, dispersed evidence intertwined with benign functionality. While static analysis can expose security-relevant signals, the central challenge is not merely identifying suspicious code, but determining whether the evidence sufficiently supports an auditable behavior-level conclusion. We formulate malware understanding as a grounded reasoning problem and argue that reliable behavior reconstruction requires three complementary forms of grounding. Domain grounding constrains how behavior hypotheses are generated and evaluated, semantics grounding localizes and connects supporting program evidence, and knowledge grounding supports behavioral attribution through externally verifiable threat knowledge. To study this hypothesis, we present Malaika, a multi-agent framework that operationalizes the three grounding mechanisms through analyst-inspired reasoning, tool-mediated evidence localization, and retrieval-based behavioral attribution. We instantiate Malaika for Android malware analysis and evaluate it on malware-understanding tasks. Results show that Malaika improves analysis quality over prior LLM-based malware-analysis frameworks and demonstrate that reliability depends not only on model capability but also on the reasoning process. In particular, comparisons against malware-analysis systems and frontier agentic frameworks show that grounding-aware reasoning produces more precise and auditable conclusions. Ablation studies further support the grounding hypothesis. These findings suggest that grounding-aware reasoning provides a principled foundation for reliable malware understanding and, more broadly, for evidence-grounded software analysis.

</details>


### [109] [Blockchain-Linked Auditable Decision Management for Telecom/IoT Fraud-Control Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09259)
*Saviz Changizi,Nasibeh Mohammadzadeh,Mohammad Shojafar,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将电信/IoT诈骗控制重构为基于区块链的可审计决策管理框架，通过QLoRA微调的大语言模型分支提升可用性，但经100,000条部署回放语料验证，低成本集中式ML集成（M1）仍是最优方案，QLoRA虽优于零样本提示但未超越M1。区块链遥测显示开销差异源于链下决策配置而非欺诈逻辑本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电信诈骗控制研究止步于检测器级分类，无法满足实际部署所需的请求级策略解析、全生命周期追溯与审计合规要求。

Method: 构建区块链链接的可审计决策管理框架：1) 确定性硬欺诈门控拦截越界请求；2) 非硬欺诈请求经集中式ML(M1)、联邦元学习(M2)或LLM(M3)三类风险源评分；3) 通过共享五态策略、两区精化机制和本地Ethereum兼容审计层解析动作；4) M3分支采用QLoRA微调提升可用性。

Result: 验证集上M1取得最优平衡（合法请求FPR 0.0890，软欺诈召回0.8341）。部署回放中M1合法FPR升至0.1646，M3-QLoRA合法FPR 0.1801（较基线的0.3915显著降低），软欺诈召回0.8240。区块链开销差异由链下决策配置驱动。

Conclusion: QLoRA微调能显著提升LLM分支的可用性，但低成本集中式集成（M1）在性能上仍占优；本研究为受控漂移回放证据而非现场部署验证。

Abstract: Telecom fraud-control studies often stop at detector-level classification, but deployment use requires request-level policy resolution, lifecycle traceability, and auditability. This paper reframes fraud control as blockchain-linked auditable decision management for synthetic telecom/IoT fraud-control requests, and its main result is that the QLoRA-tuned LLM branch becomes much more usable than zero-shot prompting but mainly approaches, rather than outperforms, a lower-cost centralized ensemble. The framework maps each synthetic deployment record to a managed request, blocks explicit out-of-boundary cases through a deterministic hard-fraud gate, scores non-hard requests using centralized ML (M1), federated meta-learning (M2), or LLM-family risk sources (M3), and resolves actions through a shared five-state policy, two-zone refinement mechanism, and local Ethereum-compatible audit layer. Evaluation uses separate synthetic training data and a 100,000-record deployment replay corpus, so the study should be read as controlled drift-replay evidence rather than field validation or proof of live deployability. On validation, M1 gives the strongest balance, with legitimate-request FPR 0.0890 under the 0.10 operating cap and soft-fraud recall 0.8341. On labeled deployment replay, however, the legitimate-FPR gap becomes large: M1 rises to 0.1646 and M3-QLoRA to 0.1801, while M3-QLoRA reduces the M3-Base legitimate FPR from 0.3915 and reaches 0.8240 soft-fraud recall. Blockchain telemetry shows that lifecycle gas, cost, latency, and throughput differences are driven by submitted off-chain decision profiles rather than changes in fraud logic.

</details>


### [110] [Leveraging Interpretable Tsetlin Machine for PDF Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09290)
*Rahul Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可解释Tsetlin机器（TM）的PDF恶意软件检测框架，通过静态分析提取特征并采用基于规则的学习进行二分类。在RIT-PDFMal-2026数据集上达到98.02%的准确率，兼具竞争性检测性能、计算效率和内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDF文件因其平台无关性与丰富功能成为广泛使用的文档格式，但也因此成为网络攻击者嵌入恶意代码的攻击向量。现有方法在检测性能与模型可解释性之间难以兼顾，亟需一种既高效又透明的检测方案。

Method: 提出基于Tsetlin Machine（TM）的可解释框架，通过静态分析从PDF文档中提取显著特征，无需执行文件；采用基于规则的学习机制对良性与恶意PDF文档进行分类。

Result: 在RIT-PDFMal-2026数据集上，该框架达到了98.02%的准确率，优于多种传统机器学习分类器及现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在检测性能、计算效率和内在可解释性三方面均表现优异，能够透明地解释分类决策，是实际PDF恶意软件检测中具有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: In the digital era, Portable Document Format (PDF) is one of the most widely used file formats for storing and exchanging digital documents due to its platform independence and rich functionality. However, these same capabilities have also made PDF files an attractive attack vector for cyberattackers, who embed malicious code within seemingly legitimate documents to compromise target systems. This paper presents a novel interpretable Tsetlin Machine (TM)-based framework for PDF malware detection. The proposed framework extracts salient features from PDF documents through static analysis without executing the files and employs rule-based learning to accurately classify benign and malicious PDF documents. Numerical evaluation on the RIT-PDFMal-2026 dataset demonstrates that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance, attaining an accuracy of 98.02% compared with several ML classifiers and existing methods. Moreover, the proposed framework provides intrinsic interpretability by transparently explaining its classification decisions. The combination of competitive detection performance, computational efficiency, and intrinsic interpretability makes the proposed framework a promising solution for practical PDF malware detection.

</details>


### [111] [SFDS: Selective File Disclosure System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09370)
*Aditya Mitra,Quazi Fariha Tasnim,Hristina Mihajloska Trpcheska*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SD-JWT标准的架构，用于安全共享只读文件。该架构通过将加密签名和完整性保护直接嵌入共享资源中，实现了可验证的真实性，无需依赖传统的身份与访问管理（IAM）基础设施（如集中式用户数据库、认证服务和授权机制），从而简化部署并保持强安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络资源访问控制依赖认证机制和IAM系统，存在复杂性高、安全风险（漏洞、配置错误、实现缺陷）等问题。同时，不同文件格式采用不同的数字签名标准（如PDF的PAdES），缺乏统一的标准化机制。

Method: 基于选择性披露JSON Web令牌（SD-JWT）标准，设计了一种将加密签名和完整性保护直接嵌入共享资源的架构。

Result: 所提出的架构消除了对复杂IAM基础设施（集中式数据库、认证服务、授权机制）的依赖，同时为不可变资源的分布提供了可验证的真实性和强安全保证。

Conclusion: 该方案简化了部署，通过直接嵌入签名和完整性保护实现了不依赖传统IAM基础设施的安全文件共享，适用于只读文件的受控分发场景。

Abstract: Access control to networked resources has been a longstanding challenge. The conventional solution relies on authentication mechanisms, which introduce additional complexities associated with Identity and Access Management (IAM). Such systems require user authentication, identity management, and authorization services, while also introducing security risks arising from vulnerabilities, misconfigurations, or implementation flaws. Furthermore, different file formats employ different mechanisms for ensuring authenticity and integrity through digital signatures. For example, PDF documents support the PDF Advanced Electronic Signature (PAdES) standard, whereas plain text files typically lack a standardized mechanism for embedding digital signatures. This paper proposes an architecture based on the Selective Disclosure JSON Web Token (SD-JWT) standard for securely sharing read-only files. The proposed architecture embeds cryptographic signatures and integrity protection directly into the shared resource, providing verifiable authenticity without relying on complex IAM infrastructures, such as centralized user databases, authentication services, or authorization mechanisms. By eliminating these components, the proposed solution simplifies deployment while maintaining strong security guarantees for the distribution of immutable resources.

</details>


### [112] [Federated Learning Architecture: Data Privacy and System Security Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09391)
*Cagdas Karatas,Hibanur Karadogan,Ahmet Yasin Ertug,Busra Buyuktanir,Kazim Yildiz,Gozde Karatas Baydogmus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探索了同态加密与差分隐私在联邦学习中的集成应用，在不显著影响模型准确性的前提下增强数据隐私安全，并在三个真实数据集上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中模型更新过程仍可能泄露敏感信息，亟需在不牺牲模型性能的前提下增强隐私保护机制。

Method: 集成同态加密（支持密文计算）与差分隐私（防止个体信息从模型输出中提取），并在Framingham、Pima Indians Diabetes和Bank Marketing数据集上测试，分析了数据异质性对模型性能的影响。

Result: 增强隐私保护的同时模型准确性未显著下降；数据异质性影响模型性能，但通过合理选择差分隐私参数、训练设置及使用更大规模数据集可提升联邦学习效率。

Conclusion: 隐私保护与高性能的人工智能系统可在医疗和金融等敏感领域安全应用。

Abstract: This study explores the integration of homomorphic encryption and differential privacy techniques to enhance data privacy and security in Federated Learning (FL) systems. FL allows data to remain on local devices, eliminating the need for centralized data collection; however, sensitive information may still be leaked during model updates. To address this issue, homomorphic encryption enables computations on encrypted data, while differential privacy prevents the extraction of individual information through statistical techniques applied to model outputs. The proposed architecture was tested on the Framingham, Pima Indians Diabetes, and Bank Marketing datasets, revealing that enhanced privacy can be achieved without significantly compromising model accuracy. Furthermore, the impact of data heterogeneity among clients on model performance was analyzed, and it was concluded that strategies such as the careful selection of differential privacy parameters and training settings, along with the use of larger datasets, can improve the efficiency of FL. The findings demonstrate that privacy-preserving and high-performance artificial intelligence systems can be securely applied in sensitive domains such as healthcare and finance.

</details>


### [113] [Triggering Stealthy Feature Map Backdoors via Physical Fault Injection in Embedded Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09473)
*Steyn Hommes,Vincent Dankbaar,Tanguy Stekke,Xiaomeng Wang,Lisanne Weidmann,Senna van Hoek,Durba Chatterjee,Lejla Batina,Zhuoran Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨层级攻击方法，将实现级物理故障注入与算法级对抗攻击相结合，通过精确故障注入操纵寄存器值，实现基于特征图的物理触发后门攻击，在ARM Cortex-M4上通过电磁故障注入验证了有效性，并指出该攻击可规避传统输入空间后门防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对嵌入式神经网络实现的故障注入攻击主要关注通过破坏权重或中间计算来诱导误分类，忽略了其与算法级对抗威胁的交互。作者旨在弥合实现级物理故障与算法级对抗攻击之间的鸿沟，揭示硬件与算法交叉层面的新型攻击向量。

Method: 1) 提出精确故障注入方法，可靠地将目标寄存器值操纵为可控状态；2) 利用该精度，提出端到端的特征图级后门攻击，将物理诱导的中间扰动作为隐蔽触发器；3) 该触发器仅在物理故障下激活，正常运行时良性；4) 在ARM Cortex-M4上使用电磁故障注入对卷积神经网络进行实证验证。

Result: 成功在嵌入式NN平台上实现了物理触发的后门攻击，该攻击对现有假设输入空间触发器的后门防御保持有效，验证了跨层级攻击的实用性和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一种位于硬件与算法交叉层面的新型攻击向量，强调了跨抽象层级部署防御的必要性。

Abstract: Fault injection (FI) attacks on embedded neural network (NN) implementations primarily focus on inducing misclassification by corrupting weights or intermediate computations, overlooking their interaction with algorithmic adversarial threats. In this work, we present a cross-level attack that bridges implementation-level physical faults to algorithm-level adversarial attacks. By characterizing fault-induced data perturbations during NN inference, we connect FI with backdoor learning, enabling system-level attacks that jointly exploit implementation- and algorithm-level vulnerabilities. Specifically, we propose a precise fault-injection method that reliably manipulates targeted register values to tractable states during execution. Leveraging this level of FI precision, we propose a novel end-to-end feature map-level backdoor attack, where physically induced intermediate perturbations serve as stealthy triggers. Unlike conventional input-based backdoors, our trigger is activated only under physical faults, causing the NN to exhibit adversarial behavior that compromises system integrity while remaining benign during normal operation. We demonstrate that such physically triggered backdoors can be mounted on embedded NN platforms and remain effective against existing backdoor defenses that typically assume input-space triggers. We showcase the attack practicality using electromagnetic FI on convolutional neural networks implemented on ARM Cortex-M4 microcontroller, which is a common platform for constrained embedded applications. Our results highlight a novel attack vector at the intersection of hardware and algorithmic levels, stressing the need for defenses across abstraction levels.

</details>


### [114] [Portable Acceleration of Learning With Errors KEMs for Post-Quantum Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09541)
*Tiziana Liberati,Nitin Shukla,Simone Rizzo,Elisabetta Boella,Matteo Barbieri,Gabriella Bettonte,Daniele Gregori,Marco Pedicini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于OpenMP Target offloading的便携式GPU实现，用于LWE-based KEM（后量子密码学中的密钥封装机制），可在NVIDIA和AMD GPU上运行。实验表明，该方法相比多核CPU基线实现了显著加速，其中NVIDIA GH200和AMD MI300X表现最佳，并指出内存系统组织和CPU-GPU交互比峰值计算能力更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码学（PQC）的过渡需要能够满足实际应用计算需求的实现。LWE-based KEM虽然安全性基础强，但矩阵运算和大规模安全随机数生成导致计算成本高昂。现有GPU实现多依赖CUDA特定优化，缺乏跨平台可移植性，存在供应商锁定问题。

Method: 使用OpenMP Target offloading实现基于普通LWE的KEM的便携式GPU实现，采用单一源代码即可在NVIDIA和AMD加速器上执行。在不同GPU架构上进行性能基准测试、运行时分析、可扩展性分析和能耗测量。

Result: OpenMP Target offloading相比多核CPU基线实现了显著加速，同时保持了跨异构GPU生态系统的源代码级可移植性。跨平台分析发现NVIDIA GH200和AMD MI300X对该内存密集型工作负载表现最佳。性能分析表明内存系统组织和CPU-GPU交互比峰值计算能力更重要。

Conclusion: 便携式GPU加速可以显著降低PQC的计算开销，同时避免供应商锁定，有助于部署量子抗性加密基础设施。该方案为异构GPU生态系统下的后量子密码学实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The transition to post-quantum cryptography (PQC) is driving demand for implementations that can meet the computational requirements of real-world applications. Among the proposed PQC constructions, Learning With Errors (LWE) based key encapsulation mechanisms (KEMs) are particularly attractive due to their strong security foundations, but they incur substantial computational costs from matrix operations and large-scale cryptographically secure random number generation. These characteristics position GPU acceleration as an effective approach for lowering the computational overhead of lattice based cryptographic schemes. In this work, we present a portable GPU implementation of a plain LWE based KEM using OpenMP Target offloading. Unlike most existing GPU implementations, which rely on CUDA specific optimizations, our approach uses a single source code base that executes on both NVIDIA and AMD accelerators. We evaluate the proposed implementation on different accelerator architectures, analyzing performance benchmarking, runtime profiling, scalability analysis, and energy to solution measurements. Experimental results show that OpenMP Target offloading delivers substantial acceleration over a multicore CPU baseline while preserving source level portability across heterogeneous GPU ecosystems. Cross platform analysis identifies NVIDIA GH200 and AMD MI300X as the most effective platforms for this memory bound workload, while profiling indicates that memory system organization and CPU GPU interaction play a more critical role than peak compute capability alone. These findings demonstrate that portable GPU acceleration can significantly reduce the computational overhead of PQC while avoiding vendor lock in, thereby facilitating the deployment of quantum resistant cryptographic infrastructures.

</details>


### [115] [VEXAIoT: Autonomous IoT Vulnerability EXploitation using AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09653)
*Katherine Swinea,Kshitiz Aryal,Lopamudra Praharaj,Maanak Gupta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VEXAIoT，一个基于LLM的多智能体框架，用于自动化IoT漏洞发现与利用。该框架由漏洞检测智能体和攻击执行智能体组成，在IoTGoat和Metasploitable环境中10种攻击场景下达到95%总体成功率，平均执行时间低于2分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统因硬件受限、固件过时和不安全默认配置而存在固有安全漏洞，现有LLM智能体在渗透测试和CTF环境中展现出潜力，但尚未应用于IoT特定漏洞的自动化安全测试。

Method: 提出VEXAIoT自主多智能体框架，包含漏洞检测智能体（负责侦察和漏洞发现）和攻击执行智能体（负责规划攻击序列并执行利用），结合LLM推理与 offensive 安全工具，在IoTGoat和Metasploitable环境中针对映射到OWASP IoT漏洞的10种攻击场景进行评估。

Result: 在260次攻击执行中，总体成功率达95.0%（IoTGoat 94.5%，Metasploitable2 96.7%），部分场景达100%成功率，token开销低，大多数攻击平均执行时间低于2分钟。

Conclusion: 结果表明LLM驱动的智能体在受控环境中自动化IoT漏洞评估和 offensive 安全工作流方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems are inherently vulnerable due to constrained hardware, outdated firmware, and insecure default configurations, creating a need for scalable and adaptive security testing approaches. While recent adoptions of Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated promise in penetration testing and Capture-the-Flag (CTF) environments, their application to IoT specific vulnerabilities remains unexplored. This paper presents an autonomous multi-agent framework, referred to as Vulnerability EXploitation using AI Agents (VEXAIoT), for vulnerability discovery and exploitation in IoT environments using LLM-based reasoning and offensive security tools. The framework combines a vulnerability detection agent and an attack execution agent to perform reconnaissance, plan attack sequences, and execute exploits against vulnerable IoT services. The system is evaluated in IoTGoat and Metasploitable environments across ten attack scenarios mapped to OWASP IoT vulnerabilities. Experimental results show attack success rate of up to 100% with low token overhead and average execution times under two minutes for most attacks. Across 260 attack executions, VEXAIoT achieves a 95.0% overall success rate, including 94.5% success in IoTGoat and 96.7% success in Metasploitable2. These results demonstrate the potential for LLM-driven agents to automate IoT vulnerability assessment and offensive security workflows in controlled environments

</details>


### [116] [Impact of Benign Connectivity Variations on Intrusion Detection for Encrypted OPC UA Traffic in Industrial Private 5G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09659)
*Song Son Ha,Florian Foerster,Henry Beuster,Tim Kittel,Dominik Merli,Gerd Scholl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了工业私有5G网络中，由合法连接变化（如控制平面活动）引发的虚假正报问题，表明即使没有攻击，机器学习入侵检测系统的异常评分也会显著升高，强调了在解读IDS输出时必须考虑控制平面上下文的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于机器学习的入侵检测系统被广泛用于监控加密工业通信，但其在真实的私有5G运行条件下的行为尚未被充分理解。

Method: 在工业私有5G网络中，针对加密的OPC UA流量，通过实验引入良性的连接变化事件，观察ML-based IDS的假阳性率和异常评分变化，并分析其与控制平面活动的时间关联性。

Result: 合法的连接事件会显著增加IDS的假阳性活动；IDS异常评分升高频繁与控制平面活动的时间段重合。

Conclusion: 在工业私有5G环境中解释IDS输出时，必须考虑控制平面上下文。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML)-based intrusion detection systems (IDSs) are increasingly used to monitor encrypted industrial communication. However, their behavior under realistic private 5G operating conditions remains insufficiently understood. This paper investigates the impact of benign connectivity variations on ML-based IDSs for encrypted Open Platform Communications Unified Architecture (OPC UA) traffic in industrial private 5G networks. Experimental results show that legitimate connectivity events can noticeably increase false positive activity despite the absence of attacks. Furthermore, elevated IDS anomaly scores frequently coincide with periods of control-plane (CP) activity associated with these events. The findings highlight the importance of considering CP context when interpreting IDS outputs in industrial private 5G environments.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Deep Gaussian Processes on Directed Acyclic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09645)
*Federico L. Perlino,Oliver Hamelijnck,Adam M. Johansen,Theodoros Damoulas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向无环图（DAG）上的深度高斯过程，提出了结构化变分近似方法，在理论上分析了先验坍缩行为和图拓扑对信息保持的影响，并在多种实际任务中取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中的许多过程可表示为DAG上的函数组合，但函数测量存在噪声、异质性采样等问题，给重建、不确定性传播和推理带来挑战。现有方法难以同时处理这些挑战，因此需要一种能够保留图依赖关系、保持组合不确定性的建模框架。

Method: 在DAG结构上对函数施加先验，自然导出DAG上的深度高斯过程；理论上分析先验坍缩行为、图拓扑和中间观测对信息保持的影响，推导了输入区分度保持的渐进频率下界；提出结构化变分近似方法，保留图依赖关系和组合不确定性，并捕获碰撞节点（collider）的"解释消除"行为。

Result: 在理论上获得了几乎必然的渐进频率下界，识别出适用该下界的广泛核函数类别；在实验中，于潜变量碰撞DAG、蛋白质信号网络和多保真重离子碰撞仿真任务上均达到最优性能，同时恢复低保真度贡献并提供可解释性。

Conclusion: DAG上的深度高斯过程能有效处理图结构函数组合中的噪声、异构采样和不确定性传播问题，理论结果和实验验证共同证明了该方法的有效性和可解释性优势。

Abstract: Many real-world processes can be represented as compositions of functions along a directed acyclic graph (DAG). In causal modelling, these correspond to the underlying mechanisms; in engineering, to multiple fidelity levels; and in gene-regulatory networks, to transcription factors. These functions are partially observed across the DAG, with noisy and heterogeneously sampled measurements, posing significant challenges for reconstruction, uncertainty propagation, and inference. To tackle these challenges, we place priors over functions and naturally arrive at Deep Gaussian Processes over DAGs. We theoretically study their prior-collapse behaviour, and the effect of graph topology and intermediate observations on the preservation of information. We obtain almost-sure lower bounds on the asymptotic frequency of depths at which the distinction between inputs is preserved, identify broad kernel classes for which these hold, and prove an observation by \cite{dunlop2018} on the role of input connections. We offer a structured variational approximation that retains graph dependencies, preserves compositional uncertainty, and captures the explaining-away behaviour of colliders. Finally, we empirically validate our theoretical results and our methodology, and model a latent-collider DAG, a protein signalling network, and a multi-fidelity heavy-ion collision emulation task, attaining state-of-the-art performance while recovering low-fidelity contributions and yielding interpretability of the simulator hierarchy.

</details>


### [118] [EHR-MPC: Inference-Time Control for Sepsis Treatment with Generative Patient Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08793)
*Joshua Pickard,Wei Qi,Na Li,Ann Woolley,Lisa Cosimi,Roy Kishony,Deborah Hung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出EHR-MPC框架，通过生成式电子健康记录模型构建患者数字孪生，将学习患者动态与优化治疗解耦，利用模型预测控制在推理时通过模拟优化脓毒症治疗方案，在多中心ICU数据集上取得了与强化学习相当的离线性能以及更优的模拟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在脓毒症治疗中学习固定策略，无法在推理阶段适应变化的临床目标，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出EHR-MPC框架，解耦患者动态学习与治疗优化：先训练生成式EHR模型作为患者数字孪生以预测临床轨迹，再通过模型预测控制（MPC）在推理时对模拟结果进行规划来优化治疗。

Result: 在覆盖8家医院的Mass General Brigham多中心ICU脓毒症队列上，EHR-MPC在离线策略重要性采样评估中达到与RL基线相当的性能，在基于策略的模拟评估中表现更优。

Conclusion: 将脓毒症治疗优化重新定义为对学习到的患者动态进行推理时控制的问题，建立了基于生成式临床模型的决策通用框架。

Abstract: Sepsis is a leading cause of mortality, yet optimal treatment policies remain contested. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) approaches learn fixed strategies for sepsis treatment, limiting adaptability to changing clinical objectives during inference. We propose EHRMPC, a framework that decouples learning patient dynamics from optimizing treatment by training a patient digital twin in the form of a generative electronic health record (EHR) model. The digital twin predicts clinical trajectories under interventions and enables model predictive control (MPC) to optimize treatments via inference-time planning over simulations. We evaluate EHR-MPC on a multicenter ICU sepsis cohort spanning 8 hospitals in the Mass General Brigham health system using both off-policy importance sampling and on-policy simulation-based evaluation. Relative to RL baselines, EHR-MPC achieves comparable off-policy performance and improved simulation performance. Unlike RL, this work frames sepsis treatment optimization as inference-time control over learned patient dynamics, establishing a general framework for decision making with generative clinical models.

</details>


### [119] [Influence Diagnostics in High-dimensional M-estimation: Precise Asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09250)
*Hugo Cui*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在高斯设计下，针对凸M估计，研究了高维极限（n ≍ d）中留一法影响力（leave-one-out influence）的分布特性，证明其收敛于一个可精确表征的极限分布，并揭示了有影响力的样本倾向于靠近决策边界这一现象，与主动学习中的标准数据选择启发式方法一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的低维大样本极限下，留一法影响力的统计特性已被充分理解，但在高维场景中，单个样本的影响力会与其他所有训练样本产生复杂的依赖关系，现有理论尚不完善。本文旨在高维极限下刻画影响力的分布特性。

Method: 在Gaussian设计下，考虑凸M估计问题，在高维极限 n ≍ d 下，证明训练集上影响力的分布收敛于一个可精确表征的极限测度。

Result: 成功刻画了高维极限下影响力的极限分布，并发现高影响力样本倾向于靠近决策边界。

Conclusion: 本文的理论结果不仅揭示了高维中影响力分布的非平凡特性，还为主动学习中“选择靠近决策边界的样本”这一常见启发式方法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The impact of a given training point on a statistical model is classically measured through its leave-one-out influence, which quantifies the effect of its removal from the training set on the model accuracy. While the statistics of leave-one-out influences are well understood in the low-dimensional, large sample limit $n\to \infty, d=O(1)$, they become more intricate in high dimensions, as the influence of a given sample develops non-trivial dependencies on all other training samples. For convex M-estimation under Gaussian design, in the high-dimensional limit $n\asymp d$, we show that the distribution of the influences across the training set converges to a limiting measure which we sharply characterize. Building on these results, we provide evidence that influential samples tend to lie close to the decision boundary, thereby making contact with a standard data selection heuristic in active learning.

</details>


### [120] [Spectrally Deconfounded Gradient Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09371)
*Andrea Nava,Peter Bühlmann,Fabio Sigrist*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于梯度提升的非线性谱解混框架，通过谱损失与正则化（特别是早停）的交互作用来缓解隐混淆问题，并提供了混合模型解释和贝叶斯调参方法，在合成和真实实验中表现出更好的可扩展性和目标函数估计效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵活的机器学习方法容易受到隐混淆的影响，即模型可能学习到由未观测混杂因子引起的伪关联而非稳定信号。现有的谱解混方法主要局限于线性模型，缺乏针对非线性模型（如梯度提升）的有效解混框架。

Method: 1) 将普通平方误差损失替换为谱损失，在混淆对齐方向上减缓学习速度；2) 通过谱收缩与正则化（尤其早停）的交互实现解混；3) 提供混合模型解释，将LAVA型收缩与随机效应调整联系起来；4) 利用经验贝叶斯过程调整谱损失超参数；5) 使用拉普拉斯近似和核随机效应将方法扩展到一般似然和非线性混淆场景。

Result: 在合成和真实实验中，谱解混梯度提升在隐混淆下改进了目标函数的估计，并且比现有的非线性谱解混基线方法具有显著更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 谱损失本身不足以实现解混，必须与正则化（特别是早停）协同作用；所提出的框架不仅适用于线性模型，还能有效扩展到非线性梯度提升场景，并且在大规模数据上具有实用优势。

Abstract: Flexible machine-learning methods can be sensitive to hidden confounding: they may learn associations induced by unobserved confounders rather than stable signals. Spectral deconfounding mitigates this problem by shrinking high-variance directions of the covariate matrix that, under dense confounding, carry latent confounder information. Existing work has largely focused on linear models. We develop a nonlinear spectral deconfounding framework for gradient boosting. Our approach replaces the ordinary squared-error loss by a spectral loss, which alters the boosting dynamics by slowing down learning in confounding-aligned directions. We show that deconfounding is not achieved by the spectral loss alone, but by the interaction between spectral shrinkage and regularization, especially in terms of early stopping. Moreover, we provide a mixed-model interpretation that connects LAVA-type shrinkage to random-effects adjustment and yields an empirical-Bayes procedure for tuning the spectral loss. We also extend the method to general likelihoods and nonlinear confounding using Laplace approximations and kernel random effects. Across synthetic and real-world experiments, spectrally deconfounded boosting improves estimation of the target function under hidden confounding and is substantially more scalable than existing nonlinear spectral deconfounding baselines.

</details>


### [121] [Characterization of the basin of convexity for multi-snapshot spike deconvolution via variable projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09593)
*Meghna Kalra,Maxime Ferreira Da Costa,Kiryung Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究多快照脉冲反卷积问题，提出变量投影公式(VarProSD)，通过消除幅度参数将问题简化为仅关于脉冲位置的非凸最小二乘。利用Beurling-Selberg极值近似，刻画了目标函数凸性盆地的显式条件，证明了估计量在多快照下的一致性，并给出了局部收敛保证。数值实验验证了理论结果及ESPRIT初始化+梯度优化的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多快照场景下从含噪卷积信号中恢复稀疏脉冲位置的问题，传统方法需要同时优化位置和幅度导致计算复杂，且缺乏对优化景观几何性质的深入理解。

Method: 采用变量投影(VarPro)技巧将幅度参数以闭式消去，得到仅关于位置的非凸最小二乘目标函数(VarProSD)；利用Beurling-Selberg极值近似推导凸性盆地的显式条件；在盆地内建立一致性和误差界理论，并证明梯度下降的局部收敛性。

Result: 给出了VarProSD目标函数凸性盆地与PSF功率谱密度、平滑度、采样带宽及脉冲间隔之间的显式关系；证明了随机噪声下多快照估计量的一致性以及对抗噪声下基于局部Lipschitz性质的更紧误差界；验证了ESPRIT初始化+梯度下降的实用有效性。

Conclusion: VarProSD框架为多快照脉冲反卷积提供了理论严谨且计算高效的解决方案，Beurling-Selberg近似成为分析此类结构化矩阵条件数的有力工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of multi-snapshot spike deconvolution, where the goal is to recover the locations of sparse impulses from their noisy convolution with a known point spread function (PSF) across multiple snapshots. We adopt a variable-projection formulation that eliminates the amplitudes in closed form, reducing the task to a nonconvex least-squares problem over the spike locations alone, which we refer to as the variable-projection formulation of spike deconvolution (VarProSD). We provide an explicit characterization of the basin of convexity of the VarProSD objective in terms of key PSF properties, including its power spectral density and smoothness, revealing how sampling bandwidth and spike separation influence the local geometry. Within this basin, we establish that the estimator is consistent in the number of snapshots under stochastic noise, and provide a complementary, sharper error bound under adversarial noise via the local Lipschitz property of the inverse map. We further show local convergence guarantees for gradient descent when initialized within the basin. A central ingredient throughout is the use of Beurling--Selberg extremal approximations, which enable sharp, PSF-agnostic bounds on the conditioning of the structured matrices arising in the optimization landscape. Numerical experiments validate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of modified ESPRIT initialization followed by gradient-based refinement.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Geometric planted matchings in high dimensions: The power of multiple views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09026)
*Timothy L. H. Wee,Kaylee Y. Yang,Zhou Fan,Cheng Mao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了高维空间中点云匹配问题的相变阈值。在单视图模型中，当噪声参数b<2时，无法恢复任何非平凡匹配；而多视图模型（K≥3）可将恢复阈值降低至b>K/(K-1)，从而突破单视图不可能性壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维点云匹配问题的信息论极限，特别是噪声阈值b=2是否为"全有或全无"的相变点，并研究多视图观测能否突破这一限制。

Method: 理论分析：证明单视图模型在b<2时任何估计量都无法恢复正比例的匹配，且欧氏距离估计不比忽略对应关系更好。多视图模型：提出多项式时间算法，证明当b>K/(K-1)时可恢复所有相对匹配至多o(n)误差。

Result: 单视图b=2阈值具有全有或全无特性；多视图模型将可恢复阈值降低至K/(K-1)，例如三视图可在3/2<b<2区间实现高效恢复。

Conclusion: 多视图观测能有效突破单视图点云匹配的不可恢复性阈值，为高维匹配问题提供了新的理论界限和实用算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering the correspondence between a collection of $n$ points in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and a noisy, permuted version of those points. In the high-dimensional regime $d=ω(\log n)$, under a Gaussian model with noise variance $σ^2=d/(b\log n)$, prior work identifies $b=2$ as the threshold for almost exact recovery. We prove that this threshold is all-or-nothing: for every fixed $b<2$, no estimator recovers a positive fraction of the matching, and even estimating the matched point cloud in Euclidean distance is asymptotically no better than ignoring the correspondence. On the other hand, we consider a multi-view generalization of the problem where $K$ noisy, independently permuted copies of the same latent point cloud are observed. Here we show that a simple polynomial-time procedure recovers all relative matchings up to $o(n)$ errors whenever $b>K/(K-1)$. Thus multiple views can break the impossibility barrier $b=2$ for the original matching problem: in particular, for $3/2 < b < 2$, the two-view model has no nontrivial recovery, but a third view makes all latent correspondences efficiently recoverable.

</details>


### [123] [Central Limit Theorems for Functionals of Persistence Diagrams in Germ-Grain Random Set Models with Applications to Goodness-of-Fit Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09228)
*Vesna Gotovac Đogaš,Marcela Mandarić*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了M-有界持续图泛函的中心极限定理，证明了在特定条件下（如相关指数衰减），这些拓扑摘要随观测窗口扩大呈现渐近正态性，并应用于检测空间相互作用（聚类或排斥）的拟合优度检验，最后在乳腺组织组织学图像中验证了方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为基于持续同调的空间点过程分析提供严格的渐近理论基础，使持续图泛函能够用于统计推断，特别是拟合优度检验以区分不同空间相互作用模式。

Method: 基于标记点过程的稳定化方法，建立M-有界持续图泛函的中心极限定理；利用持续图的矩形划分和泛函摘要（APF、提升区域支撑函数）构造检验统计量。

Result: 在相关指数衰减条件下，持续图泛函满足渐近正态性；基于所提检验统计量能有效区分聚类、排斥等不同空间模型。

Conclusion: 该方法为空间点过程拓扑数据分析提供了统计推断工具，并在乳腺组织组织学图像分析中展示了实用价值。

Abstract: This paper establishes a central limit theorem (CLT) for functionals of $M$-bounded persistence diagrams arising from germ-grain random set models. Building on stabilisation methods for marked point processes, we show that, under certain conditions, these topological summaries exhibit asymptotic normality as the observation window increases, particularly for models with exponential decay of correlations. These results are applied in goodness-of-fit tests designed to detect spatial interactions such as clustering or repulsion. Using test statistics derived from rectangular partitions of persistence diagrams and functional summaries (e.g., the APF or the support function of the lift zonoid), the study distinguishes between different models. Finally, the methodology is applied to histological images of breast tissue.

</details>


### [124] [The Cost of Discretization in Functional Linear Regression: Minimax Rates and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09350)
*T. Tony Cai,Yicheng Li*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量对函数线性回归中，当每条协变量曲线仅通过有限个带噪声的点观测时，在两种采样方案（独立随机设计和公共等距网格设计）下的极小极大估计与预测风险，推导了匹配的上下界，并构造了自适应估计器。结果表明预测风险由全观测基准项和离散化/协方差估计代价项组成，在公共设计下还额外包含网格离散误差和特征值识别成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中协变量函数往往只能在有限个带噪声的点上观测，而非完整曲线。现有研究多假设曲线完全观测或采样分辨率足够高，缺乏对有限分辨率下估计与预测风险的系统刻画。本文旨在精确刻画轨迹数n和轨迹内采样点数m如何共同影响极小极大风险。

Method: 在固定三角特征基下工作，假设协方差特征值以速率α衰减，斜率函数具有Sobolev光滑度s。分别推导了独立随机设计和公共等距网格设计下的极小极大上下界，并构造了基于协方差尺度筛选和块状预测能量阈值化的数据自适应估计器。

Result: 独立随机设计下预测风险率为n^{-(2α+2s)/(2α+2s+1)} + (nm)^{-(2α+2s)/(4α+2s+1)}；公共设计下为n^{-(2α+2s)/(2α+2s+1)} + (nm)^{-(2α+2s)/(4α+2s+1)} + m^{-(2α+2s)} + m^{-4α}。自适应估计器可在未知特征值和光滑度指标下达到这些速率。

Conclusion: 揭示了独立设计下依赖采样分辨率的尖锐相变，以及公共设计下更丰富的相图。数值模拟和真实数据验证了理论发现。

Abstract: We study scalar-on-function linear regression when each covariate curve is observed only through finitely many noisy point evaluations. Our goal is to characterize the minimax estimation and prediction risks as joint functions of the number of trajectories $n$ and the within-trajectory resolution $m$. Working in a fixed trigonometric eigenbasis, with covariance eigenvalues decaying at rate $α$ and slope function of Sobolev smoothness $s$, we derive matching minimax upper and lower bounds under two canonical sampling schemes.
  Under an independent random design, the minimax prediction rate is $n^{-\frac{2α+2s}{2α+2s+1}} + (nm)^{-\frac{2α+2s}{4α+2s+1}}$. The first term is the fully observed functional linear regression benchmark, while the second term captures the cost of noisy point evaluations after amplification by the inverse covariance operator. Under a common design on an equally spaced grid, the shared sampling geometry introduces additional obstructions, and the minimax prediction rate becomes $n^{-\frac{2α+2s}{2α+2s+1}} + (nm)^{-\frac{2α+2s}{4α+2s+1}} + m^{-(2α+2s)} + m^{-4α}$. Here the third term represents discretization error induced by the fixed grid, whereas the fourth reflects the cost of identifying unknown eigenvalues from observations on a common grid.
  We further construct data-driven adaptive estimators that screen the covariance scale and threshold blockwise prediction energy, attaining these rates without prior knowledge of the eigenvalue sequence or the smoothness indices. The results reveal a sharp phase transition that depends on the sampling resolution under independent design and a richer phase diagram under common design. Numerical simulations and a real data example illustrate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [125] [Tests for Increasing Convex Ordering Based on Generalized Tsallis Entropy Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09418)
*Aritra Saha,Siddhartha Chakraborty,Md. Zafar Anis*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种不完全熵度量（不完全加权累积残差熵、不完全累积残差Tsallis熵及其加权版本），引入相关偏序关系，探讨其与已知随机序的联系，并基于这些特征开发了用于随机相等性检验的非参数检验方法，通过模拟验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不完全累积残差熵（ICRE）及其相关检验方法存在局限性，需要更一般化的不完全熵度量框架来刻画随机变量的不确定性，并开发更有效的非参数检验方法。

Method: 1) 定义了不完全加权累积残差熵（IWCRE）、不完全累积残差Tsallis熵（ICRTE）及其加权版本（IWCRTE）；2) 引入了基于这些度量的偏序关系，并建立了与经典随机序的联系；3) 基于上述特征构造了用于随机相等性检验的非参数检验统计量；4) 推导了检验的渐近性质，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估有限样本表现。

Result: 所提出的非参数检验方法在各种替代模型和样本量下均具有良好的有限样本表现，与Zardasht (2015)基于ICRE的检验相比具有竞争力或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 本文建立的不完全熵度量框架及基于其的检验方法为随机序检验提供了更灵活的工具，丰富了熵度量在统计推断中的应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we study several incomplete entropy measures, namely the Incomplete Weighted Cumulative Residual Entropy, the Incomplete Cumulative Residual Tsallis Entropy and its weighted version, and introduce the associated partial orderings. Their connections with certain well-known stochastic orderings are also investigated. Based on these characterizations, a class of nonparametric tests for stochastic equality against ordered alternatives is developed. The asymptotic properties of the proposed tests are derived, while their finite-sample performances are assessed through extensive Monte Carlo simulations under various alternative models and sample sizes. These tests are further compared with the test based on incomplete cumulative residual entropy proposed by Zardasht (2015).

</details>


### [126] [Near-optimal node-private community estimation in polynomial-time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09441)
*Laurentiu Marchis,Olga Klopp,Po-Ling Loh,Ilias Zadik*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多项式时间、节点私密的算法，用于随机块模型中的精确恢复，几乎匹配了指数量级算法的性能，解决了Klopp & Zadik (2026)的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人工作（Klopp & Zadik, 2026）中指数时间节点私密算法虽然性能优异，但计算效率低；本文旨在设计一个多项式时间算法，在近似相同效用下实现隐私保护。

Method: 构建了惩罚似然函数的显式Lipschitz代理函数，并设计了一种精心的接受-拒绝算法，用于在多项式时间内从对应指数机制中采样社区标签。

Result: 在社区数K随节点数n对数增长的条件下，隐私参数ε增长为log(n)时，算法实现了精确恢复的极小化最优速率，匹配了隐私代价的下界。

Conclusion: 该算法在隐私、运行时间和效用上均具有理论保证，表明差分隐私在这一设置下的代价可以被有效控制到最优。

Abstract: In this paper, we resolve an open question of Klopp & Zadik (2026) by providing a high-probability polynomial-time, node-private algorithm which nearly matches the performance of their exponential-time node-private algorithm for exact recovery in stochastic block models. Our result involves an explicitly constructed Lipschitz surrogate for the penalized likelihood function, as well as a carefully devised accept-reject algorithm that samples community labels from the corresponding exponential mechanism in polynomial-time. We rigorously analyze the privacy, runtime, and utility of our proposed algorithm, showing that even when the number of communities K grows logarithmically with the number of nodes n, we can achieve the minimax rates for exact recovery with the privacy parameter epsilon growing as log(n), thus matching known lower bounds on the cost of privacy for this setting.

</details>


### [127] [Misspecified regressions with mixed regressors: robust inference and causal interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09536)
*Mengsi Gao,Peng Ding*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的统计框架，同时处理回归中的随机和固定成分，允许回归函数设定错误，并将其应用于完全随机实验中的平均处理效应估计，涵盖独立数据和聚类数据情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计框架要么假设随机回归元要么假设固定回归元，但无法涵盖实践中常见的混合情况（如随机化处理与非随机化固定协变量共存的实验）。作者旨在统一这两种文献，为混合回归元提供理论基础。

Method: 首先建立同时包含随机和固定成分的估计方程的一般理论，然后将其应用于分析可能设定错误的线性回归模型，特别关注完全随机实验。从独立数据推广到聚类数据。

Result: 建立了混合回归元条件下回归系数和标准误的因果解释理论，即使模型设定错误仍能保证因果推断的有效性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架弥合了随机回归元和固定回归元两种传统假设之间的鸿沟，为实践中常见的混合回归元场景提供了严谨的统计推断基础。

Abstract: For analytic convenience, existing statistical frameworks either assume random or fixed regressors. However, it is a little awkward that they do not cover the practical case of estimating the average treatment effect in experiments with randomized treatments and non-randomized, fixed pretreatment covariates. We unify the literature by providing the theory for regressions with mixed regressors that contain both random and fixed components. Importantly, our theory allows for misspecification of the regression functions. We first establish general results for estimating equations with both random and fixed components and then use it to analyze misspecified linear regression, with applications to completely randomized experiments. We focus on the causal interpretation of the regression coefficients and standard errors even when the models are wrong. We start with the theory for independent data and then extend the discussion to clustered data.

</details>


### [128] [High-Dimensional Interpolators Can Be Fragile: Heavy Tails and High-Dimensional Large Deviations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09547)
*Youheng Zhu,Yiping Lu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文利用大偏差方法研究高维线性插值（ridgeless回归）的尾部风险，发现其虽然期望风险可控，但右尾衰减速率仅为O(n log n)，远慢于岭回归的O(n²)速率，表明插值模型在统计上是脆弱的，即使平均表现良好也可能出现罕见但严重的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中高维插值模型的期望预测风险已被广泛研究，但其尾部风险（罕见严重错误的概率）尚未被充分理解。在运筹学和随机决策中，罕见估计误差可能产生不成比例的下游影响，因此除了平均性能外，尾部行为同样重要。

Method: 采用大偏差（large-deviation）理论方法，对高维线性插值器（ridgeless回归）与岭回归（ridge-regularized）估计量进行对比分析。通过推导尾部衰减速率来量化罕见风险事件的概率。

Result: Ridgeless回归的风险呈现重尾行为：期望风险可控，但上尾衰减速率仅为O(n log n)（n为样本量）；而岭回归的右尾偏差衰减速率为O(n²)。两者之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 正则化不仅影响偏差-方差权衡，还影响罕见高风险事件的频率。插值模型即使平均准确度高，在统计上仍可能是脆弱的，因此需要关注其尾部风险。

Abstract: High-dimensional interpolation is common in modern machine learning, but its tail risk is less understood than its expected prediction risk. Existing theory shows that interpolating models can perform well in expectation, yet such guarantees do not determine the probability of rare, severe errors. In operations research and stochastic decision-making applications, rare estimation errors can have disproportionate downstream effects, so tail behavior matters alongside average performance.
  We study the fragility of high-dimensional linear interpolators using large-deviation methods. We focus on ridgeless regression and compare it with ridge-regularized estimators. We first show that the risk of ridgeless regression can exhibit heavy-tailed behavior: although its expected risk may remain well controlled, its upper tail can decay much more slowly than that of regularized alternatives. We then quantify this phenomenon at the level of large-deviation rates. In the regime we study, ridge regularization suppresses fixed right-tail deviations at the $n^2$ scale, whereas ridgeless regression has only $n\log n$-scale decay, where $n$ is the sample size. This gap shows that interpolation can be statistically fragile even when it is accurate on average. Thus regularization affects the frequency of rare, high-impact risk events in addition to the usual bias-variance tradeoff.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Adaptive Bayes exactly tracks information over intrinsic time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08789)
*Akshay Balsubramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明贝叶斯更新和乘法权重更新的遗憾值满足精确的信息核算恒等式：每轮超额损失等于不确定性支付的即时费用加上到比较器的信息距离缩减。累积支付定义了路径依赖的"内在时间"。该分解是精确等式而非上界，因此有利的随机或低噪声场景表现为内在时间的自限性质。该演算统一涵盖Hedge、乐观变体、连续先验、Boosting、在线凸优化、上下文赌博机及重复博弈等多种算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示贝叶斯和乘法权重更新背后统一的信息论机制，将遗憾分析从松散的上界转化为精确的路径依赖等式，从而更清晰地理解算法在不同场景下的行为差异。

Method: 提出信息核算恒等式，将每轮超额损失分解为不确定性支付和KL散度缩减两项，并定义"内在时间"作为路径依赖不确定性度量；通过一阶平衡求和导出两种精确的累积遗憾分解形式。

Result: 得到精确而非上界的遗憾分解，使得有利的随机或低噪声场景可自然地通过内在时间的自限性质体现，无需在悲观最坏情况分析中留有松弛。

Conclusion: 该信息核算框架提供了一个统一视角，涵盖从Hedge到在线凸优化、上下文赌博机等广泛算法，表明这些算法的路径级行为遵循相同的信息论会计原则。

Abstract: Bayesian and multiplicative-weights updates reweight experts, models, or actions from sequential feedback. We show that the regret of any such update obeys an exact information-accounting identity. On each round, the learner's excess loss to any chosen comparator is the sum of an immediate payment for the uncertainty exposed by the round and a reduction in the information distance from the learner's current weights to the comparator. The cumulative payment defines a pathwise uncertainty clock, the \emph{intrinsic time} of the realized sequence. Summing one-step balances yields two exact adaptive decompositions of cumulative regret, one for each natural way of composing the update across rounds. Because the decompositions are exact rather than upper bounds, favorable stochastic or low-noise regimes appear as self-bounding properties of the realized intrinsic time, not as slack in worst-case analyses. The same calculus covers Hedge, optimistic and side-information variants, continuous priors, boosting, online convex optimization, contextual bandits, and repeated games: the pathwise account is the same in every case.

</details>


### [130] [NL-PAC: Specification Ambiguity and Certified Minimax Risk Floors in LLM-Mediated Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08961)
*Berkay Anahtarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自然语言PAC（NL-PAC）框架，揭示当语言模型监督信号允许多种解读时，增加标注无法解决识别问题，并基于模型阈值化解码律定义了可容许标签与候选目标集，通过概率度量标注空间直径，证明在目标盲监督下所有学习者的最坏情况风险至少为该直径的一半，且数据无关策略可达精确随机极小化最优风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常被用于对自然语言描述的任务进行标注、评估和反馈，但当任务描述存在多种合理解读而监督通道未指明具体解读时，增加标注样本仅能减少抽样误差，却无法解决因解读歧义导致的识别问题（identification problem）。现有方法未系统处理这一根本性困难。

Method: 提出NL-PAC框架，利用固定模型的阈值化解码律定义可容许标签和候选目标；定义点态可容许目标类的直径（即多个标签同时可容许的概率）；推导目标盲监督下极小化最坏情况风险的下界，并证明随机极小化最优风险可由数据无关策略达到；通过有限样本置信界从留出无标签输入中认证这些量。

Result: 在冻结的Qwen 2.5-3B模型的审计中，一个预指定提示获得正的模型相对证书（positive model-relative certificate），而一个改写提示和精确规则控制均产生零证书；保留的桥接审计发现，提供的候选解读子句无法满足将证书转移至连贯解读所需的可容许性条件。

Conclusion: NL-PAC框架的保证仅限于被审计的模型、提示、阈值和输入分布；将其扩展至人类解读需要外部验证。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly provide labels, evaluations, and feedback for tasks specified in natural language. When a specification admits multiple readings but the supervision channel does not reveal which is operative, additional labels reduce sampling error without resolving the resulting identification problem. We introduce Natural Language PAC (NL-PAC), a framework that uses a fixed model's thresholded decoding law to define admissible labels and candidate targets. The probability that multiple labels are admissible equals the diameter of the pointwise-admissible target class, and under target-blind supervision every learner incurs worst-case risk of at least half this diameter, at every sample size; the exact randomized minimax risk over this class is attained by a data-independent strategy. Finite-sample confidence bounds make these quantities certifiable from held-out unlabeled inputs. In a frozen Qwen~2.5--3B audit, one prespecified prompt yields a positive model-relative certificate, whereas a paraphrase and exact-rule controls yield zero. A held-out bridge audit finds that supplied candidate reading clauses fail the admissibility condition needed to transfer the certificate to coherent readings. The guarantee is specific to the audited model, prompt, threshold, and input distribution; extending it to human interpretations requires external validation.

</details>


### [131] [Stochastic Linear Bandits with Partially Observed Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08971)
*Gautam Dasarathy,Vineet Gattani,Lalit Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机线性赌博机中动作向量仅部分坐标可观测的问题，提出TOFU-POV算法，利用动作向量的低内在维度实现√T遗憾界，并设计了无需已知内在维度的自适应算法，通过理论下界和实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推荐系统和医疗等实际场景中，获取完整动作描述的成本高昂甚至不可能，导致传统线性赌博机中次线性遗憾在信息论上不可行。本文旨在克服这一障碍，探索在部分观测条件下如何实现有效的序贯决策。

Method: 提出TOFU-POV算法，包含三个关键步骤：(1) 利用掩码动作估计潜在动作子空间；(2) 使用逐轮冻结的表示对当前动作进行插补；(3) 在得到的低维坐标上运行OFUL算法。此外还设计了无需已知内在维度的秩自适应算法。

Result: 理论上证明了TOFU-POV实现√T遗憾界，遗憾随内在动作子空间维度而非环境维度缩放，并量化了缺失率、决策集大小和子空间条件的影响。通过新颖的乘积构造给出了匹配的下界。合成和真实数据实验验证了TOFU-POV显著优于自然基线方法。

Conclusion: 部分观测下的线性赌博机问题可以通过利用动作向量的低内在维度来克服信息论障碍；TOFU-POV算法有效实现了这一思想，且秩自适应版本无需预知内在维度。

Abstract: The stochastic linear bandit, where actions are represented as vectors and rewards are linear, is a central paradigm for sequential decision making. We study a partially observed variant of this problem in which the learning agent only sees a random subset of coordinates for each action. Such partial observability arises naturally in settings like recommendation and healthcare, where full action descriptions can be expensive or even impossible to obtain. In general, this makes sublinear regret information-theoretically impossible. However, we show that this barrier can be overcome when the action vectors have low intrinsic dimension. We propose an algorithm, TOFU-POV, that estimates the latent action subspace using the masked actions, imputes current actions using an epoch-wise frozen representation, and runs OFUL in the resulting low-dimensional coordinates. Our theory shows that TOFU-POV enjoys a $\sqrt{T}$ regret that scales with the intrinsic action subspace dimension as opposed to the ambient dimension and quantifies the interaction between these quantities and the missingness, decision set size, and subspace conditioning. We also devise a rank-adaptive algorithm that does not require the knowledge of the intrinsic dimension. We complement these guarantees with a lower bound based on a novel product construction that separates usual reward-learning uncertainty from a missingness-dependent cost intrinsic to partial observation. Synthetic and real data experiments support our theory and show that TOFU-POV can substantially improve upon natural baselines in this challenging problem.

</details>


### [132] [Group Invariant Spectral Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08987)
*Yeari Vigder,Paulina Hoyos,David Thong,Joakim andén,Joe Kileel,Amit Moscovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将对称性直接融入谱嵌入的亲和核中，证明在紧李群对称下，三种不变核构建的图拉普拉斯算子逐点收敛到商空间上的二阶微分算子，有效降低了数据维度并提高了收敛速度，在SO(2)和SO(3)对称数据集上验证了该方法能恢复数据内在几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际数据集具有旋转等对称不变性，但标准谱嵌入方法将对称相关的数据点视为无关，未能利用这一结构信息。

Method: 将对称性直接融入用于谱嵌入的亲和核中，分析了黎曼数据流形M在紧李群G对称下的三种不变核，证明其构建的图拉普拉斯算子逐点收敛到商空间M/G上的显式二阶微分算子。

Result: 理论分析表明有效维度按群维度降低，收敛速度提高；在SO(2)和SO(3)对称数据集上，G不变谱嵌入恢复了数据内在几何结构，而标准谱嵌入即使在无限数据极限下也无法做到。

Conclusion: G不变谱嵌入方法能有效利用数据对称性，降低有效维度并提升收敛性能，在对称数据集上显著优于标准方法。

Abstract: Spectral embedding methods are widely used for dimensionality reduction and clustering of high-dimensional datasets with intrinsic low-dimensional structures. Although many datasets of practical interest exhibit invariance under symmetries such as rotations, standard spectral embedding methods do not account for this, treating symmetry-related data points as unrelated. Our approach to this problem is to incorporate the symmetries directly into the affinity kernels used for spectral embedding. We analyze the case of a Riemannian data manifold $M$ with symmetries given by a compact Lie group~$G$ and prove that, under suitable conditions, graph Laplacians constructed from three types of invariant kernels converge pointwise to explicit second-order differential operators on the quotient space $M/G$. Our analysis implies improved convergence rates, as the effective dimension drops according to the dimension of the group. We validate our approach on datasets with $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ or $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ symmetry, and show that $G$-invariant spectral embedding recovers the intrinsic geometry of the data, in contrast to standard spectral embedding, which fails to do so even in the limit of infinite data.

</details>


### [133] [Optimal Top-$k$ Identification from Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08979)
*Motti Goldberger,Nils Rudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对固定置信度下基于含噪成对比较的top-k识别问题，提出了一种渐近最优的自适应比较分配算法，该算法通过在线学习最优分配策略，在δ→0时达到信息论下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定置信度纯探索问题中，许多场景已存在渐近最优算法，但基于潜效用模型的成对比较top-k识别问题（包括top-1）尚未有渐近最优算法。

Method: 首先刻画下界结构并将其转化为鞍点问题，利用该结构设计计算高效的原偶过程在线学习渐近最优比较分配，进而构建自适应的比较分配算法跟踪该分配，并证明其渐近最优性。

Result: 提出了一种在δ→0时渐近最优的自适应比较分配算法，其期望样本复杂度匹配信息论下界。

Conclusion: 填补了潜效用模型下成对比较top-k识别问题渐近最优算法的空白，为相关领域提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the active learning problem of fixed-confidence top-$k$ identification from noisy pairwise comparisons. In this problem, an algorithm sequentially chooses pairs of items to compare, observes the outcomes, and stops when it can return the set of top-$k$ items with error probability at most $δ$. The objective is to design such a $δ$-correct procedure that minimizes the expected number of comparisons (the sample complexity). This problem falls within the broader literature on fixed-confidence pure exploration in bandit models, where a common target is asymptotic optimality: the algorithm's expected sample complexity matches the information theoretic lower bound as $δ\to 0$. Asymptotically optimal procedures have been developed for a range of fixed-confidence pure-exploration problems, however to the best of our knowledge, for top-$1$, or more generally top-$k$ identification from pairwise comparisons under latent utility models an asymptotically optimal algorithm has not been established. In this setting, we develop such an algorithm. We characterize the structure of the lower bound and formulate it as a saddle-point problem. This structure enables a computationally efficient primal-dual procedure that learns the asymptotically optimal comparison allocation online. We then construct an adaptive comparison-allocation algorithm that tracks the allocation learned by the primal-dual procedure and prove it is asymptotically optimal.

</details>


### [134] [Similarity search generalisation in contrastive learning with InfoNCE loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09405)
*Nick Whiteley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了InfoNCE对比学习损失函数的群体风险与softmax相似度搜索之间的理论关系，并提出了一个基于Gâteaux微分的泛化误差上界，揭示了负样本数量k对泛化稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习训练的嵌入模型广泛用于相似性搜索，但InfoNCE损失的有限负样本行为及其泛化性能的理论理解尚不充分。现有解释多关注k→∞极限下的互信息视角或对齐-均匀性视角，缺乏对有限k情况下损失函数与搜索性能关系的量化分析。

Method: 1) 通过理论分析证明含k个负样本的InfoNCE群体风险以O(1/k)的速率逼近一个期望交叉熵，该交叉熵量化了学习嵌入函数在未见数据上的softmax相似度搜索与正样本生成器中隐含相似性的理想softmax搜索之间的偏差。2) 利用Gâteaux微分推导InfoNCE损失的连续性界，该界保留了损失函数中对负样本取平均的结构，并引入可调"逆温度"参数以匹配算法温度。3) 对于Lipschitz连续的嵌入函数，展示负样本平均效应如何随k增长而稳定泛化误差。

Result: 建立了InfoNCE有限负样本情况与softmax相似度搜索性能之间的量化联系（O(1/k)逼近）；提出了保留负样本平均结构的泛化连续性界；证明了增大负样本数量k可稳定泛化误差。

Conclusion: InfoNCE损失不仅具有互信息和均匀性-对齐性等极限解释，其有限负样本行为直接关联到相似度搜索的偏差；负样本平均效应同时体现在损失函数和泛化误差的稳定中，为对比学习实践中使用大量负样本提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Similarity search is a primary application of embedding models trained by contrastive learning. For one of the most popular contrastive learning loss functions, InfoNCE, we show that the population risk with $k$ negative samples is $O(1/k)$ close to an expected cross-entropy which quantifies deviation between i) a softmax similarity search over unseen data using the learned embedding function, and ii) an idealised softmax search over the same data but using similarity implicitly represented in the positive sample generator. This complements existing interpretations of InfoNCE in the $k\to\infty$ limit which are phrased in terms of mutual information, and alignment versus uniformity in embeddings. To quantify generalisation performance, we introduce a new continuity bound for the InfoNCE loss, obtained via Gâteaux differentiation. The bound preserves the structure of averaging over negative samples present in the loss function and features an ``inverse temperature'' parameter which can be tuned to account for the algorithmic temperature. For embedding functions which are Lipschitz in a parameter, this yields a simple demonstration that the averaging effect of $k$ negative samples in the InfoNCE loss carries over to stabilisation of the generalisation error as $k$ grows.

</details>


### [135] [Neural Collapse Is Forbidden: Information Floors in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09487)
*Bruno Abrahao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新解释了语言模型表示中的类内方差，认为它并非神经崩溃的不完全表现，而是信息存储的分配，并遵循一定规律。研究发现，宏观类别结构仅占表示方差的4-12%，而词元上下文占79-91%。理论上，词级权重衰减将下一个词预测转化为不平衡的K类问题，并证明了类内离散度至少与条件互信息 I(token; context | category) 成正比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统观点——语言模型表示中的类内方差通常被视为不完全的神经崩溃。作者认为这实际上是信息存储的分配，并试图揭示其背后的规律。

Method: 通过无量纲方差份额分析14个模型；提出中心恒等式证伪等角紧框架假设；从理论上分析词级权重衰减的影响，证明类内离散度下界与条件互信息的关系。

Result: 宏观类别结构仅占表示方差的4-12%，词元上下文占79-91%，跨100倍参数范围保持稳定；身份离散度（而非总方差）与条件互信息成正比；预训练过程中类别份额呈现过冲-衰减-部分恢复的模式。

Conclusion: 语言模型表示中的类内方差不是噪声或不完全压缩，而是有结构的信息存储，其分配遵循信息论定律，主要服务于上下文信息的编码而非类别区分。

Abstract: Within-class variance in language-model representations is commonly read as incomplete neural collapse. We argue it is allocated information storage, and that the allocation obeys a law. A one-line centering identity voids a family of simplex equiangular-tight-frame claims, including our own earlier ones; in dimensionless variance shares across 14 models, macro-category structure carries only 4-12% of representational variance and within-token context carries 79-91%, stable across a 100x parameter range. On the theory side, token-level weight decay penalizes a category in proportion to its type count, not its occurrence mass, reducing next-token prediction to an imbalanced K-class problem whose optimum orders category norms by type count. A converse floor, proved for binary categories, forces within-category dispersion to be at least proportional to the conditional mutual information I(token; context | category). The law holds: identity dispersion, not total variance, tracks this information across every tested model and partition, under a model-free estimate and even across models, where one model's information predicts another's dispersion; and over pretraining the category share overshoots, decays, and partially recovers, because the information it must carry never left.

</details>


### [136] [Statistically Undetectable Backdoors in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09532)
*Andrej Bogdanov,Alon Rosen,Neekon Vafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一种在白盒场景下统计不可检测的后门攻击方法，攻击者可以在深度前馈神经网络中植入后门，使得带后门模型与诚实训练模型在总变差距离上难以区分，同时后门能够为每个输入生成基于不变性的对抗样本，而诚实模型在多项式时间内无法生成任何此类对抗样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示模型训练者与模型使用者之间的根本能力不对称性：训练者可以植入统计上不可检测的后门，而使用者即使在白盒条件下也无法发现或利用该后门。

Method: 利用标准密码学假设，在深度前馈神经网络中植入后门，使得带后门模型与诚实模型在总变差距离上接近（白盒统计不可检测），后门通过不变性机制为每个输入提供对抗样本。

Result: 理论上证明了后门的统计不可检测性以及诚实模型无法在多项式时间内生成对抗样本；初步实证结果支持了理论发现。

Conclusion: 模型训练者拥有比模型使用者更强的能力，这种不对称性对AI安全具有重要影响，即使用户拥有模型完整权重也无法检测后门。

Abstract: We show how an adversarial model trainer can plant backdoors in a large class of deep, feedforward neural networks. These backdoors are statistically undetectable in the white-box setting, meaning that the backdoored and honestly trained models are close in total variation distance, even given the full descriptions of the models (e.g., all of the weights). The backdoor provides access to invariance-based adversarial examples for every input, mapping distant inputs to unusually close outputs. However, without the backdoor, it is provably impossible (under standard cryptographic assumptions) to generate any such adversarial examples in polynomial time. Our theoretical and preliminary empirical findings demonstrate a fundamental power asymmetry between model trainers and model users.

</details>


### [137] [A Unified Approach to Interpreting Knowledge Distillation for Large Language Models via Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08776)
*Qingzhuo Wang,Ruiyang Qin,Zhenxin Qin,Wen Shen,Zhihua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用交互分解（interaction decomposition）统一解释多种知识蒸馏方法，发现其共同机制是交互稀疏化（sparsification of interactions），不同方法性能差异源于处理复杂交互的能力，并据此提出复杂交互惩罚（CIP）损失函数，实验表明能持续提升多种蒸馏方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在LLM中取得了成功，但其背后的机制尚不清晰，作者希望探索不同KD方法的统一机制。

Method: 将LLM输出分数分解为多个交互（interaction）的求和，每个交互代表一组输入变量（如词）间的非线性关系，通过分析交互稀疏化来解释KD机制，并提出CIP损失函数显式惩罚复杂交互。

Result: CIP作为即插即用损失函数，在域内和分布外基准上均能一致提升多种KD方法的性能。

Conclusion: KD的共同机制是交互稀疏化，复杂交互的稀疏程度决定了不同KD方法的性能差异，CIP能有效提升蒸馏效果。

Abstract: Despite the success of knowledge distillation (KD) in Large Language Models (LLMs), the underlying mechanism behind its efficacy remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a unified approach to explore the common mechanism of various KD methods using interactions. Specifically, we decompose the output score of the LLM into the sum of numerous interactions. Each interaction represents a nonlinear relationship involving a set of input variables (e.g., words). Based on the decomposed interactions, we discover that the common mechanism underlying various KD methods is the sparsification of interactions, i.e., student models retain fewer interactions for inference while suppressing other interactions to zero effects. Furthermore, we discover that the performance variance across different KD methods arises from their capabilities in handling complex interactions. A KD method typically yields better performance if it enables the student model to achieve higher sparsity of complex interactions. Motivated by these insights, we propose a plug-and-play loss function called Complex Interaction Penalty (CIP) to explicitly enforce the sparsity of complex interactions during the distillation process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that integrating CIP consistently improves the performance of diverse KD methods on both in-domain and out-of-distribution benchmarks.

</details>


### [138] [iLENS: Interpretable LLM-Guided Mixture-of-Experts for Neuroimaging Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08778)
*Farica Zhuang,Seong Woo Han,Zixuan Wen,Shu Yang,Yize Zhao,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iLENS是一个基于大语言模型和混合专家系统的可解释生存预测框架，用于阿尔茨海默病转化预测，结合结构化神经影像与非结构化信息，提供高竞争性预测性能、患者亚型分类能力及透明的生物学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD的预测转化在前驱阶段至关重要，现有生存模型是静态预测器，可解释性有限且缺乏自然语言推理能力。

Method: 提出iLENS框架，利用LLM整合结构化神经影像测量和非结构化信息，通过混合专家系统指导专家路由进行生存预测。

Result: 展示了具有竞争力的预测性能、患者亚型分类能力，以及为路由决策提供透明、生物学依据的解释。

Conclusion: iLENS弥合了高性能生存分析与可解释临床决策支持之间的差距，为AD转化预测提供了可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Alzheimer's Disease (AD) is a complex neurodegenerative disorder that continues to impact millions of people worldwide. Predicting AD conversion during the prodromal stage remains critical for disease understanding and patient care. As such, survival models are widely used for AD risk prediction, yet they are typically static predictors with limited interpretability and no capacity for natural language reasoning. In this work, we propose iLENS, an interpretable large language model (LLM) guided framework based on mixture-of-experts (MoE) for survival prediction in AD conversion. Our approach uses LLM to synthesize structured neuroimaging measurements and unstructured information to guide expert routing. Our framework demonstrates competitive predictive performance and capability in patient subtyping. Furthermore, our framework provides transparent, biologically grounded rationales for its routing decisions, bridging the gap between high-performance survival analysis and interpretable clinical decision support.

</details>


### [139] [Signed Symmetric Quantization for Few-Bit Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08779)
*Ian Colbert,Eashan Dash,Pablo Monteagudo-Lago,Juan Amboage,Srinidhi N,Giuseppe Franco,Nicholas J. Fraser,Arun Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有符号对称量化（signed absmax grid），通过轻量级的符号选择规则将额外的可表示负值分配给主导离群值尾，在不引入零点偏移的前提下解决标准对称量化器因强制截断正离群值带来的量化误差问题，理论证明其在ℓ₂误差上条件性边界最优，实验表明在Qwen3、Llama3等模型上以零额外推理成本提升了困惑度和少样本准确率，且相比非对称量化在AMD EPYC CPU上实现了最高2.45倍吞吐提升和9%内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对称整数量化器因有符号整数表示中负值比正值多一个，固定正尺度会导致额外的负值被强制利用，进而迫使正侧离群值被截断，在低位宽下成为不可忽略的量化误差来源。非对称量化虽可通过零点偏移解决该问题，但引入运行时开销（如llama.cpp中非对称相比对称格式在AMD EPYC CPU上吞吐量降低2.45倍、内存增加9%）。因此需要在保持对称量化的高效运行时性能的同时，解决其因额外负值导致的截断误差问题。

Method: 提出有符号对称量化（signed absmax grid）：保留零零点（zero point始终为零），通过轻量级的符号选择规则（sign selection rule）将额外的可表示负值分配给主导离群值所在的尾侧（dominant-outlier tail）。理论上证明该网格在ℓ₂量化误差上条件性边界最优（conditionally bound-optimal），并证明标准对称量化器取反尺度等价于在同一有符号整数字母表上做单位零点偏移。

Result: 理论分析表明，在低位宽下88-99%的LLM权重分组满足有符号absmax网格的条件性最优条件。实验在Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Llama3系列模型上验证，相比标准无符号对称量化器，该方法在困惑度和下游少样本准确率上均有提升，且无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 有符号对称量化（signed absmax grid）是一种在运行时效率和量化精度之间取得更优权衡的量化方案：它保留了对称格式的高吞吐、低内存优势，同时通过符号选择解决了因额外负值导致的截断误差问题，理论上有条件最优保证，实践中在多种主流LLM上验证有效。

Abstract: The signed integer alphabet contains one more negative representable value than positive. Yet, by convention, the standard symmetric integer quantizer fixes its scale to be strictly positive, which assigns this extra representable value to the negative tail and can force clipping of positive outliers. In this work, we show that, at few-bit precision, such clipping is a non-trivial source of quantization error. Asymmetric quantization addresses this problem with a zero point, shifting the grid toward the observed data range; however, this flexibility is well-known to carry a runtime penalty. For example, in llama.cpp on an AMD EPYC(TM) "Turin" CPU, a 4-bit symmetric format uses up to 9% less memory with up to 2.45$\times$ higher throughput than its asymmetric counterpart. We highlight signed symmetric quantization as a third option that retains the runtime profile of symmetric quantization without the penalty of the asymmetric format: our signed absmax grid places the extra representable value on the dominant-outlier tail through a principled and lightweight sign selection rule while keeping the zero point at zero. Our theoretical analysis offers two main results. First, we establish the signed absmax grid as conditionally bound-optimal on $\ell_2$ quantization error, and show that the condition holds for 88-99% of weight groups across pre-trained large language models (LLMs) at low bit widths. Second, we show that negating the scale of a standard symmetric quantizer is analytically equivalent to a unit zero point shift on the same signed integer alphabet. We empirically validate our proposal on models from the Qwen3, Qwen3.5, and Llama3 families, and observe improvement in perplexity and downstream few-shot accuracy over the standard unsigned symmetric quantizer at no extra inference cost

</details>


### [140] [Sticky Routing: Training MoE Models for Memory-Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08780)
*Ali Kayyam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StickyMoE提出一种可微分的路由一致性损失函数，在预训练阶段惩罚相邻token间的专家切换，从而在不改变架构的前提下将MoE模型的专家切换率降低60%，同时保持可忽略的困惑度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘设备上运行时，相邻token频繁激活不同专家导致权重在慢速存储与快速内存间持续交换，造成严重的I/O开销。现有系统级和后处理方案均未在预训练阶段解决根本原因。

Method: 提出StickyMoE，设计一个可微分的路由一致性损失函数，惩罚相邻token之间的专家切换行为，鼓励路由器在语义连贯的跨度内维持相同的专家分配。该方法仅引入一个超参数lambda，无需架构改动，且从训练第一步起即可使专家表示与路由决策共同适应。

Result: 在小规模MoE语言模型上，StickyMoE将专家切换率降低高达60%，而困惑度退化小于4%，在质量-局部性帕累托前沿上优于后处理微调方法。

Conclusion: 路由的时间局部性最有效地在训练阶段注入，而非通过后处理方式；StickyMoE实现了训练时干预与性能保持之间的最佳平衡。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models activate only a sparse subset of experts per token, yet consecutive tokens frequently activate different experts -- causing constant weight swapping between slow storage and fast memory on edge devices. Existing remedies are either system-level (caching heuristics) or post-hoc (router fine-tuning), leaving the root cause unchanged during pretraining. We propose StickyMoE, a differentiable routing consistency loss that penalises abrupt expert switches between adjacent tokens, encouraging the router to maintain the same expert assignment across semantically coherent spans. StickyMoE requires no architectural changes, adds a single hyperparameter lambda, and unlike post-hoc methods, allows expert representations and routing decisions to co-adapt from the first training step. Experiments on small-scale MoE language models show that StickyMoE reduces the expert switch rate by up to 60% with less than 4% perplexity degradation, Pareto-dominating post-hoc fine-tuning on the quality-locality frontier. Routing temporal locality is most efficiently instilled at training time.

</details>


### [141] [Reward Transport: Property Control in Flow Matching via Noise-Space Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08781)
*Kehan Guo,Yili Shen,Yujun Zhou,Yue Huang,Chujie Gao,Shiyi Du,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reward Transport方法，通过在流匹配中将噪声与数据点根据分子奖励进行最优传输配对，实现可控分子生成。训练时对齐噪声空间坐标与分子属性，推理时调整该坐标即可控制生成分布，无需额外计算或奖励模型。在ZINC-250K和GuacaMol上实现logP和QED的单调控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配中的耦合通常仅被视为计算选择，本文发现它可以作为对齐接口——通过根据目标分子属性匹配噪声和数据，将可控结构直接嵌入到学习到的流场中。目的是在不依赖外部奖励模型或梯度引导的情况下实现可控生成。

Method: 引入Reward Transport方法，在训练时使用最优传输（Optimal Transport）耦合将标量噪声空间坐标与分子奖励对齐；推理时通过改变该坐标来引导生成分布，在耦合保持极限下，对该坐标进行阈值化可恢复交叉熵方法的截断奖励分布。

Result: 在ZINC-250K和GuacaMol上，扫描标量坐标可实现对logP的单调控制和对QED在其操作范围内的稳定控制。不同目标产生相反的结构响应（logP使分子变大，QED使分子变小），排除了通用尺寸偏差。该方法与无分类器引导和条件流匹配互补，且在epsilon预测扩散下的负结果明确了耦合级对齐缺失的情况。

Conclusion: Reward Transport提供了一种原则性的、可连续调节的分布级控制旋钮，无需预言机、奖励模型、梯度引导或额外计算，是对现有可控生成方法的有效补充。

Abstract: The coupling in flow matching -- the rule pairing noise vectors with data points -- is typically treated as a computational choice. We show that this coupling can instead serve as an alignment interface: by matching noise and data according to a target molecular property, it embeds controllable structure directly into the learned flow field. Building on this view, we introduce Reward Transport, which uses optimal transport coupling at training time to align a scalar noise-space coordinate with molecular rewards; at inference, varying this coordinate steers the generated distribution without requiring an oracle, reward model, gradient guidance, or additional computation. In the coupling-preserving limit, thresholding this coordinate recovers the Cross-Entropy Method's truncated reward distribution, providing a principled, continuously adjustable distribution-level control knob. Empirically, on ZINC-250K and GuacaMol, sweeping the scalar induces monotone control of logP and consistent QED control over its operating range; most tellingly, the same knob produces opposite structural responses for different targets, growing molecules for logP but shrinking them for QED, which rules out a generic size bias. The interface is complementary to classifier-free guidance and conditional flow matching, while a negative result under epsilon-prediction diffusion clarifies where coupling-level alignment is structurally absent. Code: https://github.com/KehanGuo2/reward-transport

</details>


### [142] [Director: Accelerating Distributed MoE Serving via Online Proactive Expert Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08782)
*Qianli Liu,Kaibin Guo,Zicong Hong,Peng Li,Fahao Chen,Haodong Wang,Jian Lin,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Director是一个面向MoE模型的分布式服务系统，通过预测驱动的在线专家放置优化，在多项式时间内实现(1+ε)近似比，将端到端延迟降低11-55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家放置优化方法依赖历史请求的专家激活模式，难以应对多样化和快速变化的请求模式，需要一种在线、主动的放置优化方法。

Method: 提出Director系统，包含：(1)轻量级级联预测器或低比特量化副本预测请求的专家激活模式；(2)在线迁移模块在计算密集型阶段执行迁移，实现近零停机；(3)基于松弛优化的专家放置算法，满足容量约束，多项式时间内达到(1+ε)近似比。

Result: 在Mistral、DeepSeek、Qwen等主流MoE模型上，与现有工作相比，端到端延迟降低11-55%。

Conclusion: Director通过预测驱动的在线专家放置策略，有效应对动态请求模式，显著降低MoE模型服务延迟，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Expert parallelism has become the prevailing paradigm to serve Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. Its efficiency depends on the communication and computation latencies of the GPUs, which are linked to the placement of experts in the GPUs. Existing works for optimizing expert placement focus on leveraging past requests' expert activation patterns. However, they demonstrate deficiencies facing diverse and rapidly changing request patterns, calling for an online, proactive approach. Implementing such an approach requires addressing several challenges: the uncertainty associated with incoming requests' expert activation, the cost of expert migration, and the NP-hard complexity in optimization. Therefore, we present Director, a new distributed MoE serving system that minimizes end-to-end latency via prediction-driven, online expert placement. Director uses either a lightweight cascaded predictor or a low-bit quantized replica for expert activation patterns of incoming requests. An online migration module then enacts the changes with near-zero downtime by executing migrations in compute-bound phases, keeping disruption bounded. At its core, a relaxation-based expert placement optimizer operates under capacity constraints, runs in polynomial time, and achieves a $(1+ε)$ approximation ratio. Finally, we implement a prototype and demonstrate, through extensive experiments, a reduction in end-to-end latency of $11\sim55\%$ for popular MoE models (e.g., Mistral, DeepSeek and Qwen) compared to existing work.

</details>


### [143] [LieBN: Batch Normalization over Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08783)
*Ziheng Chen,Yue Song,Rui Wang,Xiao-Jun Wu,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LieBN框架，利用李群上的左/右不变度量实现黎曼批量归一化，在9种几何结构（含SPD流形、旋转矩阵群、满秩相关矩阵流形）上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黎曼归一化方法多针对特定流形设计，或无法有效归一化流形上的样本分布，缺乏通用框架。

Method: 提出LieBN框架，利用李群天然存在的左/右不变度量进行批量归一化，提供控制黎曼均值和方差的理论保证；引入新的右不变度量，并通过矩阵幂变形扩展三种现有李群结构。

Result: 在9种几何结构（SPD流形4种、旋转矩阵群1种、满秩相关矩阵流形4种）上的实验验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: LieBN是一个通用的黎曼批量归一化框架，能在多种李群几何结构上有效工作，代码已开源。

Abstract: Manifold-valued measurements are prevalent in various machine learning tasks. Recent advances have extended Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) to operate on manifolds, accompanied by normalization techniques tailored to different geometries, collectively referred to as Riemannian normalization. However, most existing Riemannian normalization methods are either designed for specific manifolds or fail to effectively normalize manifold-valued sample distributions. To address these limitations, we propose LieBN, a framework for Riemannian Batch Normalization (RBN) over Lie groups. Our approach leverages the theoretically convenient left- and right-invariant metrics, which naturally exist in every Lie group, and provides theoretical guarantees for controlling the Riemannian mean and variance. We instantiate LieBN across nine distinct geometries: four on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, one on the group of rotation matrices, and four on the manifold of full-rank correlation matrices. Notably, among the SPD metrics, we introduce a novel right-invariant metric and extend three existing Lie group structures via matrix power deformation. Extensive experiments on different manifolds validate the effectiveness of our framework. The code is available at https://github.com/GitZH-Chen/LieBN.git.

</details>


### [144] [TSAI-MetaFraud: A Benchmark Dataset for Financial Fraud Transaction and Behavioral Risk Detection in Metaverse Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09528)
*Refat Ishrak Hemel,Ehsan Hallaji,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSAI-MetaFraud是一个多模态、多任务的基准数据集，用于元宇宙虚拟经济中的欺诈分析，整合了行为、交易和图结构信息，并提供了欺诈交易检测、跨模态节点分类、时序链接预测和弱监督欺诈检测等基准任务及基线评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集通常孤立地关注用户行为、身份验证或金融交易，缺乏多模态融合的数据资源，限制了可复现的多模态欺诈检测方法的评估与发展。

Method: 构建了TSAI-MetaFraud基准数据集，整合行为、交易和图结构信息，包含欺诈和机器人场景，定义了四项基准任务，并使用机器学习模型和图神经网络进行基线评估。

Result: 提供了基于机器学习和图神经网络的基线评估结果，验证了TSAI-MetaFraud作为多模态学习、图挖掘和欺诈分析基准的有效性。

Conclusion: TSAI-MetaFraud为元宇宙生态中的多模态学习、图挖掘、欺诈分析和可信AI研究提供了统一的基准平台，填补了现有数据集在多模态融合方面的空白。

Abstract: The emergence of metaverse platforms has created virtual economies that introduce new challenges related to fraud, bot activity, and illicit financial behavior. Despite growing interest in trustworthy metaverse analytics, existing datasets typically focus on user behavior, authentication, or financial transactions in isolation, limiting the development and reproducible evaluation of multimodal fraud detection methods. To address this gap, we present TSAI-MetaFraud, a multimodal, multi-task benchmark dataset for fraud analytics in virtual economies. TSAI-MetaFraud integrates behavioral, transactional, and graph-structured information while incorporating realistic fraud and automated bot scenarios. We define benchmark tasks including transaction fraud detection, cross-modal node classification, temporal link prediction, and weakly supervised fraud detection, and provide baseline evaluations using machine learning models and graph neural networks. By jointly capturing behavioral activity, financial interactions, and relational structure within a unified virtual economy, TSAI-MetaFraud provides a benchmark for advancing multimodal learning, graph mining, fraud analytics, and trustworthy AI in emerging metaverse ecosystems.

</details>


### [145] [HERO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Benchmark Library for Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08784)
*Thinh T. H. Nguyen,Le-Tuan Nguyen,Minh-Duong Nguyen,Nhi Trinh,Anh Tran Nam Nguyet,Dung D. Le,Kok-Seng Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HERO基准库，通过解耦任务分割、客户端数据分割和客户端任务序列三个维度，实现可比较的联邦持续学习评估。实验表明方法行为在不同异构设置下差异显著，平均准确率会掩盖弱客户端表现，任务顺序错配与同步评估偏向不同策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦持续学习评估难以比较，因为不同工作在数据集、任务分割、客户端数据分割、任务顺序、骨干网络、内存假设和报告规则上同时改变，缺乏统一的基准框架。

Method: 将通常耦合的三个选择解耦：任务分割、客户端数据分割和客户端任务序列，分别用α控制客户端数据偏斜、ρ控制任务顺序错配。在CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上评估，采用最终平均准确率、平均遗忘率和底部10%客户端准确率，并在OGB-MolPCBA上扩展领域增量学习案例。

Result: 方法行为在简单和异构设置之间显著变化；平均准确率会掩盖底部客户端的弱表现；任务顺序错配与同步评估偏向不同策略；HERO接口可推广到图像之外的领域迁移难度分析。

Conclusion: HERO作为异构感知的FCL基准库，通过解耦评估维度、标准化报告规则，支持可复现且感知设置的评估，暴露了现有方法在不同异构条件下的真实表现差异。

Abstract: Federated continual learning (FCL) evaluates how distributed clients learn from changing data streams while retaining previously learned knowledge. Existing evaluations are difficult to compare because they often change datasets, task splits, client data splits, task orders, backbones, memory assumptions, and reporting rules simultaneously. We introduce \textbf{HERO}, a heterogeneity-aware benchmark library for FCL. HERO builds benchmark streams by separating three choices that are often coupled, namely the task split, the client data split, and the client task sequence. In HERO-Core, the main comparable benchmark, $α$ controls client data skew and $ρ$ controls task-order mismatch. We evaluate representative FCL methods on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet using final average accuracy, average forgetting, and bottom-10\% client accuracy.
